// UNSELECTED SOURCE-ONLY SEEDED WIDTH1 PROPOSAL. Never compiled or run.
// All load/emit blocks are source-written: no helper or macro call boundary.
#[repr(C)]
struct FixedArrayMergeCursor<'a> {
    indices: &'a [u8; 64],
    timestamps: &'a [[u8; 8]; 64],
    lp: usize, le: usize, rp: usize, re: usize,
    li: u16, ri: u16, lt: u64, rt: u64,

            let source: &[u8; 64] = indices[0..64].first_chunk::<64>().expect("index64");

                        let out = s.li as u8;
                        s.lp += 1;
                        if s.lp < s.le {
                            let index = s.indices[s.lp];
                            s.li = index as u16;
                            if index != u8::MAX {
                                s.lt = u64::from_le_bytes(s.timestamps[index as usize]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            s.li = 256;
                        }
                        out
                    } else {
                        let out = s.ri as u8;
                        s.rp += 1;
                        if s.rp < s.re {
                            let index = s.indices[s.rp];
                            s.ri = index as u16;
                            if index != u8::MAX {
                                s.rt = u64::from_le_bytes(s.timestamps[index as usize]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            s.ri = 256;
                        }
                        out
                    }
                },
                {
                    // p5.out63: fully expanded emit expression.
                    #[cfg(test)]
                    {
                        if s.li != 256 && s.ri != 256 {
                            SORT_COMPARISONS.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
                        }
                        SORT_MOVES.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
                    }
                    let take_left = if s.ri >= 255 {
                        s.li != 256
                    } else {
                        s.li < 255 && s.lt <= s.rt
                    };
                    if take_left {
                        let out = s.li as u8;
                        s.lp += 1;
                        if s.lp < s.le {
                            let index = s.indices[s.lp];
                            s.li = index as u16;
                            if index != u8::MAX {
                                s.lt = u64::from_le_bytes(s.timestamps[index as usize]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            s.li = 256;
                        }
                        out
                    } else {
                        let out = s.ri as u8;
                        s.rp += 1;
                        if s.rp < s.re {
                            let index = s.indices[s.rp];
                            s.ri = index as u16;
                            if index != u8::MAX {
                                s.rt = u64::from_le_bytes(s.timestamps[index as usize]);
                            }
                        } else {
                            s.ri = 256;
                        }
                        out
                    }
                },
            ]
        };
        indices[0..64].copy_from_slice(&output);
    }
}
